Implementation of coordinate_update:
```
def coordinate_update(h,c,p,n,eps=1e-12,bisect_iter=40):
    h0=h[p]
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1
    s=np.zeros(L)
    ps=diag-p
    s[ps:ps+n] += 1.0 - h
    s[p:p+n] -= h[::-1]
    s[diag] = 1.0 - 2.0*h0  # ensure exactly? optional
    # arrays
    pos = s[diag+1:]? Need exclude diag and maybe zero.
    # But for linear constraints, exclude diag index. Need get indices not diag. Since diag in middle.
```

Active subgradient method details:
```
def active_subgrad(h, n, iters, deadline, tol=1e-3, lr0=1.0, name?):
    dx=2/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    for it in range(iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       c = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full')
       C = c.max()*dx
       if C < best_c: update
       maxc = C/dx
       # active weights: maybe softmax with temperature T_c = active_tol (unweighted)
       # use weights w = exp((c-maxc)/tau) for c within maxc - 5tau
       tau = max(1e-4, active_tol_unw)
       w = np.exp((c - maxc)/tau)
       w[c < maxc - 8*tau] = 0
       s=w.sum(); if s==0: w = (c==maxc); w/=w.sum()
       w/=s
       # compute g = dx*sum w_l ∂c_l/∂h
       g = zeros(n)
       diag=n-1
       # To avoid looping over all lags, loop over nonzero w (maybe active). For each q with w > small:
       lam_level = 1e-8
       idxs=np.nonzero(w > lam)(0)
       for q in idxs:
          ell=q-diag; wq=w[q]
          if ell>=0:
             r=n-ell
             g[:r] += wq*(1-h[ell:])
             g[ell:] += wq*(-h[:r])  # g[ell:n] -= wq*h[:r]
          else:
             r=n+ell # n-(-ell)? if ell=-m, r=n-m
             g[:r] += wq*(-h[-ell:])  # h[m:]
             g[r:] += wq*(1-h[:r])
       # g currently sum over lags, but weights sum1 so average already? We multiplied wq, sum 1. Good.
       g *= dx
       # step
       h = project_feasible(h - lr*g, n)
```

Let's implement coordinate_update efficiently.

Pseudo:
```
def coordinate_update(h,c,p,n,eps=1e-12,bis=38):
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1; h0=h[p]
    # maybe if h0 very close 0/1 still update if fractional could help
    s=np.zeros(L)
    ps=diag-p
    s[ps:ps+n] += 1.0 - h
    s[p:p+n] -= h[::-1]
    # ensure diag exact
    s[diag]=1.0-2.0*h0
    # Build combined linear arrays
    # To avoid allocate c_lin each update? Create arrays of length L-1?
    c_lin = np.empty(L-1)
    s_lin = np.empty(L-1)
    c_lin[:diag] = c[:diag]
    c_lin[diag:] = c[diag+1:]
    s_lin[:diag] = s[:diag]
    s_lin[diag:] = s[diag+1:]
    pos=s_lin>eps; neg=s_lin<-eps; zero=~(pos|neg)
    zmax = np.max(c_lin[zero]) if np.any(zero) else -np.inf
    pos_a = 1.0/s_lin[pos]; pos_b = c_lin[pos]*pos_a
    neg_a = 1.0/s_lin[neg]; neg_b = c_lin[neg]*neg_a
    c0_cur=c[diag]
    B=1.0-2.0*h0
    cur_max=c.max()
    # lower bound lo=max(zmax,0? c nonneg)
    lo=max(0.0, zmax)
    hi=cur_max
    # optional if hi <= lo + 1e-14 return None
    # bisection
    for _ in range(bis):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       feasible=False
       if mid >= zmax - 1e-13:
          upper=1.0; lower=0.0
          if pos_a.size:
             u = h0 + np.min(mid*pos_a - pos_b)
             if u < upper: upper=u
          if neg_a.size:
             l = h0 + np.max(mid*neg_a - neg_b)
             if l > lower: lower=l
          if upper < 0: upper=0? Actually if lower>1 or upper<0 infeasible.
          if lower <= upper + 1e-13:
             xlo=lower if lower>0 else 0.0
             xhi=upper if upper<1 else 1.0
             if xlo <= xhi + 1e-13:
                # c0 existence
                disc = B*B + 4.0*(c0_cur - mid)
                if disc <= 1e-14:
                   feasible=True
                else:
                   r=math.sqrt(disc)
                   d1=0.5*(B-r); d2=0.5*(B+r)
                   x1=h0+d1; x2=h0+d2
                   if xlo <= x1 + 1e-13 or xhi >= x2 - 1e-13:
                      feasible=True
       if feasible: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
       if hi-lo < 1e-13: break
    Lstar=hi
    if Lstar >= cur_max - 1e-12: # no improvement
       return None
    # choose x
    upper=1.0; lower=0.0
    if pos_a.size: upper=min(upper, h0+np.min(Lstar*pos_a-pos_b))
    if neg_a.size: lower=max(lower, h0+np.max(Lstar*neg_a-neg_b))
    xlo=max(0.0, lower); xhi=min(1.0, upper)
    disc=...
    if disc<=1e-14:
       x=0.5*(xlo+xhi)
    else: roots x1,x2
       if xlo <= x1:
          x=min(x1, xhi)
       elif xhi >= x2:
          x=max(x2, xlo)
       else:
          # fallback choose clamp 0.5?
          x = h0
    # clip
    x=clip(x,0,1)
    # candidate: evaluate new max exactly using s and c0 formula
    delta=x-h0
    if abs(delta)<1e-15: return None
    new_max = max(np.max(c + s*delta), c0_cur + B*delta - delta*delta) # but c+s*delta includes diag with s_diag B, then c0 formula double? For diag, c+s*delta = c0+Bδ, need -δ^2. So new_max_diag = c0_cur+Bδ-δ^2. For others c+sδ.
    # compute efficiently:
    new_c = c + s*delta
    new_c[diag] = c0_cur + B*delta - delta*delta
    new_max = new_c.max()
    if new_max <= cur_max - 1e-12:
       c[:] = new_c
       h[p]=x
       return x
    else:
       # maybe try other feasible points: endpoints/roots
       candidates = {xlo,xhi,x1,x2,0,1,h0,0.5} within [0,1]
       bestx=x; bestnew=Lstar
       for cand:
          if cand<0 or cand>1: continue
          d=cand-h0
          # update diag
          # We can compute max without allocating? For few candidates, allocate maybe.
          tmp = c + s*d
          tmp[diag]=...
          val=tmp.max()
          if val<bestnew:
             bestnew=val; bestx=cand
       if bestnew <= cur_max - 1e-12:
          c[:] = c + s*(bestx-h0); diag fix; h[p]=bestx; return bestx
       else:
          return None
```

Now active subgradient: If we include, need ensure time. Let's design robust `subgrad_improve(h,n,deadline, base_trau=2.0, max_iters=200, max_delta=0.08)`.
Pseudo:
```
def subgrad_improve(h, n, deadline, iters=100, delta=0.1):
    best=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    h=h.copy(); dx=2/n; diag=n-1
    for it in range(iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       cur=c.max()*dx
       if cur<best_c: best/h update
       maxu=cur/dx
       tau = max(0.5, 5.0*(it/(iters+1)) + 0.5)?? Unweighted.
       w=np.exp((c-maxu)/tau)
       w[c < maxu - 6*tau]=0
       sw=w.sum()
       if sw==0: w=(c>=maxu-1e-9); sw=w.sum()
       w/=sw
       g=np.zeros(n)
       # loop nonzero w if count < maybe all if tau large. Use thresh=1e-6
       idx=np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-6)
       if len(idx)>np.floor(n*0.8): # if too many, use all active? Could be expensive. Use top active sorted? maybe threshold higher.
          # Restrict to top max_active=200 largest weights
          top=np.argsort(w)[-200:]; idx=top
       for q in idx:
          ell=q-diag; wq=w[q]
          if ell>=0:
             r=n-ell
             g[:r] += wq*(1-h[ell:])
             g[ell:] -= wq*h[:r]
          else:
             m=-ell; r=n-m
             g[:r] -= wq*h[m:]
             g[r:] += wq*(1-h[:r])
       # g is weighted sum ∂c, weights sum1
       g *= dx
       # normalize direction
       d=-g
       mx=np.max(np.abs(d))
       if mx <=1e-15: break
       d = d/mx*delta
       # try factors
       accepted=False
       for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
           cand=project_feasible(h+fac*d,n)
           cv=compute_c5(cand,n)
           if cv < cur - 1e-12:
              h=cand; cur=cv; accepted=True; break
           elif cv < best_c -1e-12:
              best=cand.copy(); best_c=cv
       if not accepted:
           # maybe try random small perturbation? 
           pass
       # periodically orthogonalize? no
    return best_h,best_c
```
This evaluates compute_c5 multiple times O(n^2) per iter. 200*5*~0.5ms? 500ms? okay. But if np.correlate 700 takes 1ms, 200*5=1000ms? Actually 1000 calls *1ms =1s. Good.
Direction with dx scaling: d normalized, delta 0.1, good. It may just do hill climbing. Since active subgradient direction might not be exact due averaging, evaluating candidate ensures descent. If no factor improves, it stalls. Could add small noise to escape: if not accepted for many, h=project(h + 0.01*rand). But CD from noise maybe better.

Binary anneal: Might be more useful for global. Implement simple swap hill-climbing/anneal using full correlate. Use `random`? deterministic RandomState.
```
def binary_search(n, deadline, seed, iters=5000):
 rng=...
 h=(rng.rand(n)<0.5).astype(float)
 # adjust exact count
 while sum>target: flip random 1 to0; while sum<target flip 0 to1; or select random permutations.
 cur=compute_c5
 best=h.copy(); best_c=cur
 ones = np.flatnonzero(h==1); zeros=...
 temp=0.05; end=0.005
 for i in range(iters):
   if time>deadline: break
   # choose random p in ones, q in zeros
   p=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; q=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
   h[p]=0; h[q]=1; update arrays position
   new=compute_c5
   if new<cur: cur=new; best update; accept
   elif rng.rand() < exp(-(new-cur)/temp): accept cur=new
   else: revert
   temp = max(end, temp*0.999?)
 return best
```

We need guarantee sum==n/2 exactly. Since evaluation in task says sum(h)*dx=1 equivalently sum(h)==n/2 exactly (for discrete? maybe they check close). We can enforce by adjusting a few elements. Use:
```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
  target=n/2.0
  while True:
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
      mw=((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))
      if not mw.any(): mw=(h<1-1e-12)
      idx=np.nonzero(mw)[0]
      add=diff / len(idx)
      h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+add,0,1)
    else: similar subtract
    if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-13 and iteration>100: break
  return h
```

FISTA/gradient schedule:
```
def smooth_projected(h,n,tau, iters, lr, deadline, restart=15):
  h=h.copy(); v=zeros; F=F(h)
  for it:
    if time>deadline: break
    Fh, g = objgrad(h,tau)
    if not finite break
    y = h + vratio*v  # momentum
    Fy, gy = objgrad(y,tau) # maybe use gradient at y for FISTA
    # step
    step = -gy * lr
    y_new = project(y + step)
    Fnew=F(y_new)
    if Fnew <= Fy + 1e-12: accept; else backtracking reduce lr
    if accept:
       v_new=(t/t_next)*(y_new-h) + ( - lr*? )? FISTA momentum:
```

```
      Fc=objgrad(cand,tau).value
      if Fc < Fh - 1e-14: accept; h=cand; break
  if no accept: lr*=0.5; if lr<1e-6 break
```

```
Potential micro-optimizations:
- Preallocate `c_lin`, `s_lin` arrays? Each call allocate. Could use static buffers with length 2n-2? But top-level no global? Could define inside? No closures but can use global variables? Better keep simple.

```
def coord_update_lagr(c, h, p, n, lam, hi, eps=1e-11, bits=40, improve_tol=1e-8):
    L=2*n-1; diag=n-1; h0=h[p]
    s=np.empty(L)
    s.fill(0.0)
    ps=diag-p
    s[ps:ps+n] += 1.0 - h
    s[p:p+n] -= h[::-1]
    B_orig = 1.0 - 2.0*h0
    s[diag] = B_orig
    # linear arrays excluding diag
    c_lin=np.empty(L-1); s_lin=np.empty(L-1)
    c_lin[:diag]=c[:diag]; c_lin[diag:]=c[diag+1:]
    s_lin[:diag]=s[:diag]; s_lin[diag:]=s[diag+1:]
    a = s_lin + lam
    pos = a > eps
    neg = a < -eps
    zero = ~(pos|neg)
    if np.any(zero): zmax = c_lin[zero].max()
    else: zmax = -1e100
    if hi < zmax + 1e-13: return None
    # precompute
    pos_a = 1.0/a[pos]; pos_b = c_lin[pos]*pos_a
    neg_a = 1.0/a[neg]; neg_b = c_lin[neg]*neg_a
    c0=c[diag]; B=B_orig+lam
    # lower
    lo = max(-abs(lam)-2.0, zmax)
    # But if lo < -1 and zero negative? c nonneg; ok. Also if lo<0? okay.
    hi0=hi
    for _ in range(bits):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       feas = (mid >= zmax - 1e-12)
       if feas:
          upper=1.0; lower=0.0
          if pos_a.size:
             u = h0 + float(np.min(mid*pos_a - pos_b))
             if u < upper: upper=u
          if neg_a.size:
             l = h0 + float(np.max(mid*neg_a - neg_b))
             if l > lower: lower=l
          if lower <= upper + 1e-12:
             xlo = lower if lower>0 else 0.0
             xhi = upper if upper<1 else 1.0
             if xlo <= xhi + 1e-12:
                disc = B*B + 4.0*(c0 - mid)
                if disc <= 1e-12:
                   feas=True
                else:
                   r=math.sqrt(disc)
                   x1=h0+0.5*(B-r)
                   x2=h0+0.5*(B+r)
                   if xlo <= x1 + 1e-12 or xhi >= x2 - 1e-12:
                       feas=True
       if feas: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
       if hi-lo < 1e-12: break
    if hi >= hi0 - improve_tol: return None
    # choose x as before with B, hi (Lstar)
    upper=1.0; lower=0.0
    if pos_a.size: upper=min(upper, h0+float(np.min(hi*pos_a - pos_b)))
    if neg_a.size: lower=max(lower, h0+float(np.max(hi*neg_a - neg_b)))
    xlo = lower if lower>0 else 0.0; xhi = upper if upper<1 else 1.0
    disc = B*B + 4.0*(c0 - hi)
    if disc <= 1e-12:
       x = 0.5*(xlo+xhi)
    else:
       r=math.sqrt(disc); x1=h0+0.5*(B-r); x2=h0+0.5*(B+r)
       if xlo <= x1 + 1e-12:
          x = min(x1, xhi)
       elif xhi >= x2 - 1e-12:
          x = max(x2, xlo)
       else:
          x = h0
    if x < 0: x=0.0
    elif x > 1: x=1.0
    d = x - h0
    if abs(d) < 1e-14: return None
    # evaluate new L and max
    tmp = c + s*d  # length L alloc
    tmp_diag = c0 + B_orig*d - d*d
    if tmp_diag > tmp[diag]: tmp[diag]=tmp_diag? Actually c+s*d diag = c0+B_orig*d, need replace.
    tmp[diag] = tmp_diag
    newM = float(np.max(tmp))
    newL = newM + lam*d
    if newL <= hi0 - improve_tol*0.5:
       c[:] = tmp
       h[p] = x
       return x, newL
    # fallback candidates
    cands = (xlo, xhi, x1 if disc>0 else 0.5, x2 if disc>0 else 0.5, 0.0, 1.0, h0, 0.5)
    bestL = hi0; bestx=None; besttmp=None
    for cx in candidates:
       if cx< -1e-12 or cx>1+1e-12: continue
       cx=clip
       dc=cx-h0
       if abs(dc)<1e-14: continue
       t = c + s*dc
       t[diag]=c0+B_orig*dc-dc*dc
       v=float(t.max()) + lam*dc
       if v < bestL:
          bestL=v; bestx=cx; besttmp=t
    if bestx is not None and bestL <= hi0 - improve_tol:
       c[:] = besttmp; h[p]=bestx; return bestx,bestL
    return None
```
But in fallback, if `disc` not defined? define. Candidate list inside loop; use tuple with condition to avoid undefined. Since top-level no lambdas but okay.
```

def lambda_descent(h,n,lam_init,max_passes,deadline,drift=20.0, proj_every=10,seed=1):
  h=h.copy(); target=n/2; S=float(h.sum())

       res = coord_update_lagr(c,h,p,n,lam,M, improve_tol=1e-8)

          x, newL = res
          S += x - h[p]? Wait h[p] updated inside coord_update. Need delta before. coord_update modifies h and c but returns x,newL; old h0 lost. We can get delta? It updated h[p]=x, but S old? def active_polish(h,n,deadline,iters=200,maxdelta=0.1):
```
  best=h; best_c=cur
  for it in range(iters):
    c=correlate; cur=...
    maxc=c.max()
    # weights: active lags c >= maxc - tau*unscaled? use tau=0.5 + 10*(remaining?)
    tau= max(1e-3, 1.0 + 20* (it/iters?)?) unweighted c.
    w=exp((c-maxc)/tau); w[c<maxc-6tau]=0; normalize
    # direction g via weighted lags (dx? not needed if normalize)
    g=zeros; loop idx w>1e-5 top maybe 100; update derivative d (without dx)
    # step = -d normalized to maxdelta
    d=-g; m=abs max; if m=0 break; d=d/m*maxdelta
    # try factors and factors with small λ? just project, evaluate C
    for fac in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
      cand=project_feasible(h+fac*d,n)
      cv=compute_c5(cand,n)
      if cv<cur-1e-10: h=cand; cur=cv; break
    # if no accept, add small random perturbation to d? h=project(h+0.005*rand)
```

Project function optimized:
```
def project_feasible(u,n):
    t=n*0.5
    lo=max(u.min()-2.0, -5.0); hi=min(u.max()+2.0, 5.0)
    # ensure sum at lo >=t, hi <=t; if not adjust
    if np.sum(np.clip(u-lo,0,1)) < t: lo=-10.0
    if np.sum(np.clip(u-hi,0,1)) > t: hi=10.0
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi); s=np.sum(np.clip(u-mid,0,1))
       if s>t: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    v=np.clip(u-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    d=t-v.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-13:
       m=(v>1e-14)&(v<1-1e-14)
       if m.any(): v[m]+=d/m.sum()
       else:
          idx=(v<1-1e-14).nonzero()[0] if d>0 else (v>1e-14).nonzero()[0]
          if idx.size: v[idx[0]]=np.clip(v[idx[0]]+d,0,1)
    return v
```
Maybe if bounds lo/hi wrong. Since u in [0,1] mostly, lo=-1,hi=2 enough. Use `lo=-2.0; hi=2.0` because if all u in [0,1], at -2 sum=n, at2 sum=0. If u outside, expand: `lo=min(-2.0, u.min()-1.0); hi=max(2.0,u.max()+1.0)`. Sum at lo? if u min=-100, lo=-101, all clip 1; hi=1? Wait if u max=100, hi=101, all 0. Use that.
50 bisection enough? Range maybe 200, precision 1e-13 after 50? 200/2^50~1.8e-13. Good.
Correction may leave sum error if all saturated? target feasible, no.

Active_polish line count: Let's write concise.
```
def active_polish(h,n,deadline,iters=80,delta=0.12,seed=7):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); best=h.copy(); best_c=c5(h,n); cur=best_c
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for it in range(iters):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); mc=c.max(); cur=mc*2/n
       if cur<best_c-1e-12: best=h.copy(); best_c=cur
       tau=20.0*(it+1)/iters + 0.5
       w=np.exp((c-mc)/tau); w[c<mc-6*tau]=0.0
       sw=w.sum()
       if sw<1e-300: w=(c>=mc-1e-8); sw=w.sum()
       if sw>0: w/=sw
       idx=np.flatnonzero(w>1e-6)
       if idx.size==0: break
       if idx.size>200:
          top=np.argsort(w[idx]); idx=idx[top[-200:]]
       g=np.zeros(n)
       diag=n-1
       for q in idx:
          ell=q-diag; wq=w[q]
          if ell>=0:
             r=n-ell
             g[:r]+=wq*(1-h[ell:])
             g[ell:]-=wq*h[:r]
          else:
             r=n+ell
             g[:r]-=wq*h[-ell:]
             g[r:]+=wq*(1-h[:r])
       m=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if m<1e-15: break
       d=-g/m*delta
       # maybe add small random to escape? if it%10==9: d += 0.05*rng.normal(size=n); normalize?
       accepted=False
       for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
          cand=project_feasible(h+fac*d,n)
          cv=c5(cand,n)
          if cv < cur - 1e-12:
             h=cand; cur=cv; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          # try opposite? maybe negative half?
          for fac in (0.25,0.125):
             cand=project_feasible(h - fac*d,n) # but d already descent, opposite conservative
             cv=c5(cand,n)
             if cv < cur - 1e-12:
                h=cand; cur=cv; accepted=True; break
       # if still no accept and it%20==19: random small
       if not accepted and it % 25 == 24:
          h=project_feasible(h + 0.02*rng.normal(size=n), n)
    # final best update
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=c.max()*2/n
    if cur<best_c: best=h.copy(); best_c=cur
    return best,best_c
```
The try opposite h - fac*d is ascent? d=-grad, so -d is ascent; only try if maybe nonconvex valley? It could worsen. Better not. Use random jitter instead. def feasible_polish(h,n,deadline,pass_limit=200):
```
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); best=h.copy(); best_c=c5(h,n); cur=best_c
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); M=c.max()
    for p in range(pass_limit):

      order=np.arange(n) if p%2==0 else np.arange(n-1,-1,-1)
      accepted=0

        if (p_idx & 31)==0 and time>deadline: break
```

Let's refine `lambda_cd` deadline and pass count:
```
def lambda_cd(h,n,lam_init,deadline,max_passes=10000,drift=30.0,proj_every=8):
  h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); target=n/2; S=target
  c=...
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5(h,n)
  lam=lam_init
  rng=np.random.RandomState(abs(int(lam_init))+1)
  order=np.arange(n)
  pass_no=0; stall=0
  while pass_no<max_passes and time.time()<deadline:
    if pass_no % 7 ==0:
       order=rng.permutation(n)
    updates=0
    for idx,p in enumerate(order):
      if (idx & 63)==63 and time.time()>deadline: break
      M=float(c.max())
      res=coord_update_lagr(c,h,p,n,lam,M,commit=True,improve_tol=1e-8)
      if res is not None:
         x,delta,newL=res
         S += delta
         updates +=1
    if updates==0:
       stall +=1
    else: stall=0
    pass_no +=1
    # check feasible candidate every few passes or when projected due drift
    if (pass_no % 5 == 0) or updates==0:
       hx=project_feasible(h,n); cv=c5(hx,n)
       if cv<best_c: best_h=hx.copy(); best_c=cv
       # if drift too high: keep current unconstrained after projection? we need set h feasible to avoid uncontrolled drift
    if abs(S-target)>drift:
       h=project_feasible(h,n); S=target; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
       # best already? evaluate
       cv=c5(h,n); if cv<best_c: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cv
    # update lam
    viol=S-target
    if abs(viol)>0.5:
       step=0.25*viol
       lam += max(-4.0,min(4.0,step))
    else:
       lam *= 0.5
    if lam>50: lam=50
    elif lam<-50: lam=-50
    if stall>=6: break
  hx=project_feasible(h,n); cv=c5(hx,n)
  if cv<best_c: best_h=hx.copy(); best_c=cv
  return best_h,best_c
```

But if we want to use evaluated best for future? We continue from unconstrained h. If drift high, project and set h feasible. Good.
Should we update best_h to projected every 5, but continue h unconstrained. If unconstrained h far from feasible, next projection candidate may be poor; but λ controls. Good.

```
def feasible_polish(h,n,deadline,pass_limit=300,tests_per_pass=60):
 h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); best...; c=...; cur=...
 order=np.arange(n)
 st=0
 for pass in range(pass_limit):
   if time>deadline: break
   if pass%2: order=order[::-1]
   tested=0; improved=0
   for p in order:
      if time>deadline or tested>=tests_per_pass: break
      M=c.max()
      res=coord_update_lagr(c,h,p,n,0.0,M,commit=False,improve_tol=1e-5)
      if res is None: continue
      x,delta,_=res
      tested+=1
      hc=h.copy(); hc[p]=x
      # quick projection maybe project_feasible
      hc=project_feasible(hc,n)
      cv=c5(hc,n)
      if cv < cur - 1e-10:
         h=hc; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=cv; improved+=1
         if improved>=5: break  # maybe continue? break to restart pass after several improvements
   if improved==0: break
 # final best update
```

```
    newL = float(t.max()) + lam*d

        return (x,d,newL)
    # fallback
    bestL=hi0; bestx=None; bestt=None
    for cx in (xlo,xhi,0.0,1.0,h0,0.5,x1,x2): ...
```

Detailed code draft:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'))*2.0/n)

def project_feasible(u,n):
    t=0.5*n
    lo=min(-2.0,float(u.min())-1.0); hi=max(2.0,float(u.max())+1.0)
    if np.sum(np.clip(u-lo,0.,1.)) < t: lo=-100.0
    if np.sum(np.clip(u-hi,0.,1.)) > t: hi=100.0
    for _ in range(48):
       m=0.5*(lo+hi); s=np.sum(np.clip(u-m,0.,1.))
       if s>t: lo=m
       else: hi=m
    v=np.clip(u-0.5*(lo+hi),0.,1.)
    d=t-v.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-13:
       k=(v>1e-14)&(v<1.0-1e-14)
       if k.any():
          v[k]+=d/k.sum(); v[v<0]=0.; v[v>1]=1.
       else:
          idx=np.flatnonzero((v<0.5) if d>0 else (v>0.5))
          if idx.size: v[idx[0]]=min(1.0,max(0.0,v[idx[0]]+d))
    return v
```
Use v<0.5/d>0? If all values near 0 or1 and d small; okay.

Coord_update compact:
```
def coord_update_lagr(c,h,p,n,lam,hi,commit=True,tol=1e-5):
    L=2*n-1; dg=n-1; h0=h[p]
    s=np.zeros(L)
    a=dg-p
    s[a:a+n] += 1.0-h
    s[p:p+n] -= h[::-1]
    b0=1.0-2.0*h0; s[dg]=b0
    c_lin=np.empty(L-1); s_lin=np.empty(L-1)
    c_lin[:dg]=c[:dg]; c_lin[dg:]=c[dg+1:]
    s_lin[:dg]=s[:dg]; s_lin[dg:]=s[dg+1:]
    ss=s_lin+lam
    pos=ss>1e-11; neg=ss<-1e-11; zero=~(pos|neg)
    zmax=float(c_lin[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e100
    if hi < zmax + 1e-10: return None
    pa=1.0/ss[pos]; pb=c_lin[pos]*pa
    na=1.0/ss[neg]; nb=c_lin[neg]*na
    b=b0+lam; c0=c[dg]
    lo=max(zmax, -abs(lam)-2.0 if lam<0 else 0.0)
    # but if lo>hi? hi max c; zmax<=hi. if lam<0 lo may >hi? no.
    for _ in range(42):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi); ok=(mid>=zmax-1e-11)
       if ok:
          up=1.0; lox=0.0
          if pa.size: up=min(up,h0+float(np.min(mid*pa-pb)))
          if na.size: lox=max(lox,h0+float(np.max(mid*na-nb)))
          if lox<=up+1e-11:
             xlo=lox if lox>0 else 0.0; xhi=up if up<1 else 1.0
             if xlo<=xhi+1e-11:
                disc=b*b+4.0*(c0-mid)
                if disc<=1e-11 or xlo<=h0+0.5*(b-sqrt(disc)) if disc>0 else True or ...
```
Need write c0 condition carefully. Avoid Python conditional with undefined if. Use nested:
```
if disc <= 1e-11:
    ok=True
else:
    r=math.sqrt(disc); x1=h0+0.5*(b-r); x2=h0+0.5*(b+r)
    ok = (xlo <= x1 + 1e-11) or (xhi >= x2 - 1e-11)
```
then bisection update.
After loop:
```
if hi >= hi0 - tol: return None
up=1.0; lox=0.0
if pa.size: up=min(...)
if na.size: lox=max(...)
xlo=lox if lox>0 else 0.0; xhi=up if up<1 else 1.0
disc=...; x1=x2=0.5
if disc>1e-11:
   r=sqrt; x1=...; x2=...
   if xlo <= x1+1e-11: x=min(x1,xhi)
   elif xhi >= x2-1e-11: x=max(x2,xlo)
   else: x=0.5*(xlo+xhi)
elif xlo<=xhi: x=0.5*(xlo+xhi)
else: x=h0
if x<0: x=0.
elif x>1: x=1.
d=x-h0
if abs(d)<1e-13: return None
t=c+s*d; t[dg]=c0+b0*d-d*d; newL=float(np.max(t))+lam*d
if newL >= hi0 - 0.5*tol:
    # fallback candidates
    bestL=hi0; bestx=None; bestt=None
    for cx in (xlo,xhi,0.0,1.0,0.5,h0,x1,x2):
       if cx<0 or cx>1: continue
       dc=cx-h0
       if abs(dc)<1e-13: continue
       tt=c+s*dc; tt[dg]=c0+b0*dc-dc*dc
       v=float(np.max(tt))+lam*dc
       if v<bestL: bestL=v; bestx=cx; bestt=tt
    if bestx is None or bestL >= hi0 - tol: return None
    if commit: c[:]=bestt; h[p]=bestx
    return (bestx,bestx-h0,bestL)
else:
    if commit: c[:]=t; h[p]=x
    return (x,d,newL)
```

```
def lambda_cd(h,n,lam,deadline,max_passes=100000,drift=25.0):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); target=0.5*n; S=target
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); bh=h.copy(); bc=c5(h,n); rng=np.random.RandomState(1234+int(abs(lam)*10))
    order=np.arange(n); stall=0; pp=0
    while pp<max_passes and time.time()<deadline:
       if pp%6==0: order=rng.permutation(n)
       upd=0
       for i,p in enumerate(order):
          if (i&127)==127 and time.time()>deadline: break
          M=float(np.max(c))
          r=coord_update_lagr(c,h,p,n,lam,M,commit=True)
          if r is not None:
             S+=r[1]; upd+=1
       pp+=1
       viol=S-target
       if abs(viol)>0.2:
          lam=math.copysign(min(abs(lam)+abs(0.25*viol),40.0), lam+ (viol if abs(lam)>0 else 1.0)) # too complex
```


`active_polish` compact:

```
def active_polish(h,n,deadline,iters=80,mx=0.12,seed=9):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); best=h.copy(); bc=c5(h,n); cur=bc
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed); dg=n-1
    for it in range(iters):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); mc=float(np.max(c)); cur=mc*2.0/n
       if cur<bc-1e-12: bc=cur; best=h.copy()
       tau=0.5+18.0*(it+1)/max(1,iters)
       w=np.exp((c-mc)/tau); w[c<mc-6*tau]=0.; sw=w.sum()
       if sw<1e-300: w=(c>=mc-1e-8); sw=w.sum()
       if sw>0: w/=sw
       idx=np.flatnonzero(w>1e-6)
       if idx.size==0: break
       if idx.size>200:
          tv=w[idx]; idx=idx[np.argsort(tv)[-200:]]
          wsum=tv[-200:].sum() # after indexing? Actually tv sorted? We used argsort, tv_unsorted? Need select.
```
To top 200:
```
if idx.size>200:
    vals=w[idx]
    order=np.argsort(vals)
    idx=idx[order[-200:]]
wsum=w[idx].sum()
g=np.zeros(n)
for q in idx:
   ell=q-dg; wq=w[q]/wsum
   ...
```

```
for f in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
   cand=project_feasible(h+f*d,n); cv=c5(cand,n)
   if cv<cur-1e-11: h=cand; cur=cv; acc=True; break
if not acc and it%20==19:
   h=project_feasible(h+0.015*rng.normal(size=n),n)
```

`feasible_polish`:
```
def feasible_polish(h,n,deadline,passes=200,tests=35):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); best=h.copy(); bc=c5(h,n); cur=bc
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); order=np.arange(n); noimp=0
    for pp in range(passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if pp%2: order=order[::-1]
       tested=0; imp=0
       for p in order:
          if time.time()>deadline or tested>=tests: break
          M=float(np.max(c))
          r=coord_update_lagr(c,h,p,n,0.0,M,commit=False)
          if r is None: continue
          x=r[0]
          tested+=1
          hc=h.copy(); hc[p]=x; hc=project_feasible(hc,n)
          cv=c5(hc,n)
          if cv<cur-1e-11:
             h=hc; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cur=cv; imp+=1
             if cv<bc-1e-12: bc=cv; best=h.copy()
       if imp==0: noimp+=1
       else: noimp=0
       if noimp>=3: break
    hx=project_feasible(h,n); cv=c5(hx,n)
    if cv<bc: bc=cv; best=hx.copy()
    return best,bc
```
Could `order[::-1]` modifies view? fine.

`run`:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); dl=t0+budget_s-3
    try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h0)
    except Exception:
       n=700; h0=np.zeros(n); h0[np.random.RandomState(seed).choice(n,n//2,replace=False)]=1.0
    if n>1000:
       xo=np.linspace(0,2,n); xn=np.linspace(0,2,1000); h0=project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xo,h0),1000); n=1000
    elif n==0: ...
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=c5(h0,n)
    scale=max(0.1,min(1.0,budget_s/1100.0))
    phases=[(0,0.0,320),(1,0,150),(0,8.0,80),(1,0,40),(0,-8.0,60),(0,15.0,50),(1,0,40),(0,-15.0,40),(0,25.0,30),(1,0,30),(2,0,120)]
    for typ,val,dur in phases:
       st=time.time(); end=min(dl, st+max(5.0,float(dur)*scale))
       if end<=st or time.time()>dl-5: break
       if typ==0:
          h1,c1=lambda_cd(best_h,n,float(val),end)
       elif typ==1:
          h1,c1=active_polish(best_h,n,end)
       else:
          h1,c1=feasible_polish(best_h,n,end)
       if c1<best_c: best_h=h1.copy(); best_c=c1
    # optional complement if time
    if time.time()<dl-60*scale:
       hc=1.0-best_h; hc=project_feasible(hc,n)
       end=min(dl,time.time()+60*scale)
       h1,c1=active_polish(hc,n,end)
       if c1<best_c: best_h=h1.copy(); best_c=c1
    # final feasible if time
    if time.time()<dl-5:
       h1,c1=feasible_polish(best_h,n,dl-1,passes=100,tests=25)
       if c1<best_c: best_h=h1; best_c=c1
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c,n
```

If xhi>=x2 choose max(x2,xlo); if xlo>x2, x=xlo. If interval spans feasible both sides but xlo<=x1 true; picks x1 (left root) which may be far but feasible. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(u, n):
    t = 0.5 * n
    lo = min(-2.0, float(np.min(u)) - 1.0)
    hi = max(2.0, float(np.max(u)) + 1.0)
    if float(np.sum(np.clip(u - lo, 0.0, 1.0))) < t:
        lo = -100.0
    if float(np.sum(np.clip(u - hi, 0.0, 1.0))) > t:
        hi = 100.0
    for _ in range(48):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(u - m, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > t:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    v = np.clip(u - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - float(v.sum())
    if abs(d) > 1e-13:
        k = (v > 1e-14) & (v < 1.0 - 1e-14)
        if k.any():
            v[k] += d / float(k.sum())
            v[v < 0.0] = 0.0
            v[v > 1.0] = 1.0
        else:
            idx = np.flatnonzero(v < 0.5 if d > 0 else v > 0.5)
            if idx.size:
                v[idx[0]] = min(1.0, max(0.0, v[idx[0]] + d))
    return v

def coord_update_lagr(c, h, p, n, lam, hi, commit=True, tol=1e-5):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    dg = n - 1
    h0 = h[p]
    s = np.zeros(L)
    a = dg - p
    s[a:a + n] += 1.0 - h
    s[p:p + n] -= h[::-1]
    b0 = 1.0 - 2.0 * h0
    s[dg] = b0

    c_lin = np.empty(L - 1)
    s_lin = np.empty(L - 1)
    c_lin[:dg] = c[:dg]
    c_lin[dg:] = c[dg + 1:]
    s_lin[:dg] = s[:dg]
    s_lin[dg:] = s[dg + 1:]

    ss = s_lin + lam
    pos = ss > 1e-11
    neg = ss < -1e-11
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    zmax = float(c_lin[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e100
    if hi < zmax + 1e-10:
        return None

    pa = 1.0 / ss[pos]
    pb = c_lin[pos] * pa
    na = 1.0 / ss[neg]
    nb = c_lin[neg] * na

    c0 = c[dg]
    b = b0 + lam
    lo = max(zmax, -abs(lam) - 2.0 if lam < 0 else 0.0)
    hi0 = hi

    for _ in range(42):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        ok = mid >= zmax - 1e-11
        if ok:
            up = 1.0
            lox = 0.0
            if pa.size:
                up = min(up, h0 + float(np.min(mid * pa - pb)))
            if na.size:
                lox = max(lox, h0 + float(np.max(mid * na - nb)))
            if lox <= up + 1e-11:
                xlo = lox if lox > 0.0 else 0.0
                xhi = up if up < 1.0 else 1.0
                if xlo <= xhi + 1e-11:
                    disc = b * b + 4.0 * (c0 - mid)
                    if disc <= 1e-11:
                        ok = True
                    else:
                        r = math.sqrt(disc)
                        x1 = h0 + 0.5 * (b - r)
                        x2 = h0 + 0.5 * (b + r)
                        ok = (xlo <= x1 + 1e-11) or (xhi >= x2 - 1e-11)
        if ok:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
        if hi - lo < 1e-12:
            break

    if hi >= hi0 - tol:
        return None

    up = 1.0
    lox = 0.0
    if pa.size:
        up = min(up, h0 + float(np.min(hi * pa - pb)))
    if na.size:
        lox = max(lox, h0 + float(np.max(hi * na - nb)))
    xlo = lox if lox > 0.0 else 0.0
    xhi = up if up < 1.0 else 1.0

    disc = b * b + 4.0 * (c0 - hi)
    x1 = x2 = 0.5
    if disc > 1e-11:
        r = math.sqrt(disc)
        x1 = h0 + 0.5 * (b - r)
        x2 = h0 + 0.5 * (b + r)
        if xlo <= x1 + 1e-11:
            x = min(x1, xhi)
        elif xhi >= x2 - 1e-11:
            x = max(x2, xlo)
        else:
            x = 0.5 * (xlo + xhi)
    else:
        x = 0.5 * (xlo + xhi)
    if x < 0.0:
        x = 0.0
    elif x > 1.0:
        x = 1.0
    d = x - h0
    if abs(d) < 1e-13:
        return None

    t = c + s * d
    t[dg] = c0 + b0 * d - d * d
    newL = float(np.max(t)) + lam * d

    if newL >= hi0 - 0.5 * tol:
        bestL = hi0
        bestx = None
        bestt = None
        for cx in (xlo, xhi, 0.0, 1.0, 0.5, h0, x1, x2):
            if cx < 0.0 or cx > 1.0:
                continue
            dc = cx - h0
            if abs(dc) < 1e-13:
                continue
            tt = c + s * dc
            tt[dg] = c0 + b0 * dc - dc * dc
            v = float(np.max(tt)) + lam * dc
            if v < bestL:
                bestL = v
                bestx = cx
                bestt = tt
        if bestx is None or bestL >= hi0 - tol:
            return None
        if commit:
            c[:] = bestt
            h[p] = bestx
        return bestx, bestx - h0, bestL

    if commit:
        c[:] = t
        h[p] = x
    return x, d, newL

def lambda_cd(h, n, lam, deadline, max_passes=100000, drift=25.0):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    target = 0.5 * n
    S = target
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(1234 + int(abs(lam) * 10))
    order = np.arange(n)
    stall = 0
    pp = 0

    while pp < max_passes and time.time() < deadline:
        if pp % 6 == 0:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        upd = 0
        for i, p in enumerate(order):
            if (i & 127) == 127 and time.time() > deadline:
                break
            M = float(np.max(c))
            r = coord_update_lagr(c, h, p, n, lam, M, commit=True)
            if r is not None:
                S += r[1]
                upd += 1
        pp += 1
        viol = S - target
        if abs(viol) > 0.2:
            dlam = 0.25 * viol
            if dlam > 4.0:
                dlam = 4.0
            elif dlam < -4.0:
                dlam = -4.0
            lam += dlam
            if lam > 40.0:
                lam = 40.0
            elif lam < -40.0:
                lam = -40.0
        else:
            lam *= 0.5

        if abs(viol) > drift:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            S = target
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            cv = c5(h, n)
            if cv < best_c:
                best_c = cv
                best_h = h.copy()

        if pp % 4 == 0 or upd == 0:
            hx = project_feasible(h, n)
            cv = c5(hx, n)
            if cv < best_c:
                best_c = cv
                best_h = hx.copy()

        if upd == 0:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 8:
                break
        else:
            stall = 0

    hx = project_feasible(h, n)
    cv = c5(hx, n)
    if cv < best_c:
        best_c = cv
        best_h = hx.copy()
    return best_h, best_c

def active_polish(h, n, deadline, iters=80, mx=0.12, seed=9):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)
    cur = best_c
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    dg = n - 1

    for it in range(iters):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        mc = float(np.max(c))
        cur = mc * (2.0 / n)
        if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

        tau = 0.5 + 18.0 * (it + 1) / max(1, iters)
        w = np.exp((c - mc) / tau)
        w[c < mc - 6.0 * tau] = 0.0
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw < 1e-300:
            w = (c >= mc - 1e-8).astype(float)
            sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw <= 0.0:
            break
        w /= sw

        idx = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-6)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        if idx.size > 200:
            vals = w[idx]
            ord2 = np.argsort(vals)
            idx = idx[ord2[-200:]]
        wsum = float(w[idx].sum())
        g = np.zeros(n)

        for q in idx:
            ell = q - dg
            wq = w[q] / wsum
            if ell >= 0:
                r = n - ell
                g[:r] += wq * (1.0 - h[ell:])
                g[ell:] -= wq * h[:r]
            else:
                r = n + ell
                g[:r] -= wq * h[-ell:]
                g[r:] += wq * (1.0 - h[:r])

        mg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if mg < 1e-14:
            break
        d = -g / mg * mx
        acc = False
        for f in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            cand = project_feasible(h + f * d, n)
            cv = c5(cand, n)
            if cv < cur - 1e-11:
                h = cand
                cur = cv
                acc = True
                break
        if not acc and it % 20 == 19:
            h = project_feasible(h + 0.015 * rng.normal(size=n), n)

    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)
    if cur < best_c:
        best_c = cur
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c

def feasible_polish(h, n, deadline, passes=200, tests=35):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5(h, n)
    cur = best_c
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    order = np.arange(n)
    noimp = 0

    for pp in range(passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if pp % 2:
            order = order[::-1]
        tested = 0
        imp = 0
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline or tested >= tests:
                break
            M = float(np.max(c))
            r = coord_update_lagr(c, h, p, n, 0.0, M, commit=False)
            if r is None:
                continue
            x = r[0]
            tested += 1
            hc = h.copy()
            hc[p] = x
            hc = project_feasible(hc, n)
            cv = c5(hc, n)
            if cv < cur - 1e-11:
                h = hc
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                cur = cv
                imp += 1
                if cv < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cv
                    best_h = h.copy()
        if imp == 0:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp >= 3:
                break
        else:
            noimp = 0

    hx = project_feasible(h, n)
    cv = c5(hx, n)
    if cv < best_c:
        best_c = cv
        best_h = hx.copy()
    return best_h, best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    dl = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s)) - 3.0

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        h0 = np.zeros(n)
        rng0 = np.random.RandomState(seed)
        h0[rng0.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)] = 1.0

    if n <= 0:
        n = 700
        h0 = np.zeros(n)
    if n > 1000:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, 1000)
        h0 = np.interp(xn, xo, h0)
        n = 1000
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = c5(h0, n)

    scale = max(0.1, min(1.0, float(budget_s) / 1100.0))
    phases = [
        (0, 0.0, 320.0),
        (1, 0.0, 150.0),
        (0, 8.0, 80.0),
        (1, 0.0, 40.0),
        (0, -8.0, 60.0),
        (0, 15.0, 50.0),
        (1, 0.0, 40.0),
        (0, -15.0, 40.0),
        (0, 25.0, 30.0),
        (1, 0.0, 30.0),
        (2, 0.0, 120.0),
    ]

    for typ, val, dur in phases:
        st = time.time()
        end = min(dl, st + max(5.0, float(dur) * scale))
        if end <= st or time.time() > dl - 5.0:
            break
        if typ == 0:
            h1, c1 = lambda_cd(best_h, n, float(val), end)
        elif typ == 1:
            h1, c1 = active_polish(best_h, n, end)
        else:
            h1, c1 = feasible_polish(best_h, n, end)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1.copy()

    if time.time() < dl - 60.0 * scale:
        hc = 1.0 - best_h
        hc = project_feasible(hc, n)
        end = min(dl, time.time() + 60.0 * scale)
        h1, c1 = active_polish(hc, n, end)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1.copy()

    if time.time() < dl - 5.0:
        h1, c1 = feasible_polish(best_h, n, dl - 1.0, passes=120, tests=25)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c, n
```
